## From the maintainer

The maintainer's own projects. **This is not a ranking.** They show up in the tables above only when a source listed them on its own.

- [mjmirza/app-store-compliance](https://github.com/mjmirza/app-store-compliance) — Avoid common App Store and Google Play rejections. Open playbook and pre-submission guard with 125 documented rejection patterns and fixes, privacy manifest and Data safety checks, 117 tracked deadlines (EU AI Act, DSA, COPPA, age laws), and a skill for AI coding agents. (8,061 stars)
- [mjmirza/hetzner-mcp](https://github.com/mjmirza/hetzner-mcp) — Enterprise-grade Model Context Protocol server for the full Hetzner platform. Cloud, Storage Box, and Robot dedicated servers. Cost-guarded, security-hardened, every endpoint live-tested. (22 stars)
- [mjmirza/patterns](https://github.com/mjmirza/patterns) — Master level software patterns reference. 18 dimensions per entry, every claim cited and verified. (19 stars)
